
h_tamriel = {
	2703.1.1 = { holder = reman_100 } # Reman I
	2762.6.6 = { holder = reman_101 } # Kastav, deposted, goes into exile in black marsh (blackrose_prison)
	2805.12.11 = { holder = reman_102 } # Reman II
	2845.8.10 = { holder = reman_103 } # Brazollus Dor
	2877.2.12 = { holder = reman_104 } # Reman III
	2920.12.6 = { holder = akavir_99 } #Versidue-Shaie takes over as Potentate
	3244.2.25 = { holder = akavir_100 } #Savirien-Chorak
	3350.12.19 = { holder = 0 } #Empire and Potentate collapse after Savirien-Chorak gets murdered
	3774.1.2 = { #Tiber
		holder = septim_1
		government = administrative_government
		succession_laws = {
			dynastic_acclamation_succession_law
		}
		effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = holder
					holder = { has_government = administrative_government }
				}
				set_state_faith = faith:eight_divines
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = holder
					has_dlc_feature = royal_court
				}
				holder = {
					set_court_type = court_administrative
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = holder
					NOT = { has_dlc_feature = roads_to_power }
				}
				holder = {
					change_government = autocracy_government
				}
			}
			holder ?= {
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = { 
							has_realm_law = religious_tolerance_law_1 
						}
					}
					add_realm_law = religious_tolerance_law_1 
				}
			}
		} 
	} 
}

##Cyrodiilic Potentate
e_potentate = {
	2791.1.1 = { #Tsamien-Varyel
		holder = potentate_1
		government = administrative_government
		succession_laws = {
			dynastic_acclamation_succession_law
		}
		effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = holder
					holder = { has_government = administrative_government }
				}
				set_state_faith = faith:eight_divines
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = holder
					has_dlc_feature = royal_court
				}
				holder = {
					set_court_type = court_intrigue
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = holder
					NOT = { has_dlc_feature = roads_to_power }
				}
				holder = {
					change_government = autocracy_government
				}
			}
			holder ?= {
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = { 
							has_realm_law = religious_tolerance_law_1 
						}
					}
					add_realm_law = religious_tolerance_law_1 
				}
			}
		} 
	}
	2853.11.2 = { holder = potentate_2 } #Sidri-Ashak
	2881.5.16 = { holder = akavir_99 } #Versidue-Shaie
	3244.2.24 = { holder = akavir_100 } #Savirien-Chorak
	3350.12.18 = { holder = 0 } #Empire and Potentate collapse after Savirien-Chorak gets murdered
	3351.4.18 = { holder = chorak_101 } #Aixnieras-Ves
	3362.12.4 = { holder = 0 } #Attrebus takes IC, proclaims empire
}
